> **Zach:** *"just one freaking top to bottom scroll with sections Week 1, then the date, then the checklist items for that day."* This is it — the whole plan, nothing else to open.

*A box is checked or it is not. A checked box says when. Anything that slipped says where it came from, and the day it left says where it went.*

---

# Week 1 — Aug 17 - 23 · The Foundation

The week the FUB signal pipeline stopped being a mystery. Twenty-eight signals were proven firing live, sentries went up to catch them going quiet, and the mapping that decides what each signal MEANS became data instead of code. Zach certified it closed on 8/23.

*This week is closed — Zach certified it and its work is done. The day-by-day detail lives on [the week's page](/master-plan/week-1).*

---

# Week 2 — Aug 24 - 30 · The Cascade

The week the foundation work cascades outward. The activity catalog finishes becoming plain vocabulary, the full-surface QA walks run against the deployed build, and eight new programs Zach opened on 8/23 all start burning at once - the simulator, the showing flagship, the search console, the client dashboard, CRM foolproof, the mobile app, Myli's state stack, and the blueprint. It is the busiest week on the plan and the one everything after it stands on.

*The days for this week get cut from the plan the week before it is worked, so there is nothing to check off here yet.*

---

# Week 3 — Aug 31 - Sep 6 · The Four Targets

Zach ruling #102 (8/31): the dashboard done, the pill engine done, HouseLoop home value done, and the Myli OS completely built out - by end of Sunday Sep 6. Everything else is paused with its reason written down, never deleted. The day-by-day plan: /boards/week-roadmap.

Routes PO Box, malformed, name-only and missing-house-number to a third state instead of forcing adopt-or-mint.**
  Added 2026-09-09 via the task door. [details →](/master-plan/floor-51)

  Added 2026-09-09 via the task door. [details →](/master-plan/floor-51)
- [ ] **RESOLVER: a pure SCORED resolver is REFUSED — killed using our own instance. With plausible weights (house-number mismatch -50, same street +25, 7m +40, same ZIP +10, similar type +10) '10009 just defeated the contradiction and became 10011 again': enough weak evidence swamps one extremely important contradiction. If scoring is ever wanted it must be CONSTRAINED — hard compatibility filter first, scoring only among survivors.**
  Added 2026-09-09 via the task door. [details →](/master-plan/floor-51)
- [ ] **RESOLVER exceptions (corner lots, renumbers, rural conversions) are POSITIVE EVIDENCE, never weaker thresholds: 'house_number differs => incompatible UNLESS independent authoritative evidence establishes an address alias' via an alias/history table with source + confidence (USPS LACSLink). If no evidence, ABSTAIN rather than relax the veto.
